- **solady-ownable-init-frontrun**: Detect front-runnable ownership initialization in Solady Ownable / OwnableRoles. Solady's `_initializeOwner` is a guarded one-time setter (it reverts with `AlreadyInitialized` on a second call) but it is NOT access-controlled, so in constructor-less deployment paths (minimal-proxy clones, EIP-1167, factory `create`/`create2` without atomic init) an attacker can call the public initializer first and seize ownership. Activate on solady Ownable/OwnableRoles in clones, factories, or any non-atomic deploy+init. - **solady-safetransferlib-no-contract-check**: Detect Solady SafeTransferLib calls that assume the token has code.
